Title: Cofiant, neu Hanes Bywyd a Marwolaeth y Parch. Thomas Charles, A.B., yn ddiweddar o'r Bala, Sir Feirionydd; wedi ei gyfieithu a'i gasglu gan y Parchedig Thomas Jones; at yr hyn y chwanegwyd Marwnad ar yr achos
Creator: Thomas Jones (compiler and translator); Thomas Charles of Bala (his own papers, translated)
Publisher: Bala: argraffedig gan Robert Saunderson
Publication Date: 1816
Rights: Public Domain (published 1816, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'NOT_IN_COPYRIGHT')
Language: cym
Source: Internet Archive item cofiantneuhanes00jonegoog (https://archive.org/details/cofiantneuhanes00jonegoog), the item's own cofiantneuhanes00jonegoog_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Oxford University; host metadata date: 1816. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 074d873af6961b79a9b640e08885cde40747427d548ce97f8cbfc34797433788.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; the host's front matter up to and including the line beginning 'at |http: //books' removed (47 lines); nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Oxford University copy scanned by Google; Google's usage notice at the head is removed. The year reads '181C' in the OCR; the host's date is 1816.
Content note: The Welsh life of Thomas Charles of Bala, organiser of the revival's Sunday schools and a founder of the Bible Society, 'translated and collected' by Thomas Jones from Charles's own papers, with long passages of his diary and letters in Welsh and an elegy at the end.
